```text
4.  **Determine the parameters:**
    *   `location`: "Tokyo"
    *   `units`: "fahrenheit" (optional, but explicitly requested).
5.  **Execute the tool call.**
6.  **Formulate the response:** State the temperature clearly based on the tool's output.

*Self-Correction/Refinement:* The context provided in the prompt is a massive block of technical documentation (schema migrations, sprint retrospectives, API specs, etc.). It does *not* contain weather information. Therefore, I must rely on the `get_weather` tool and ignore the irrelevant context for this specific query.

*Action:* Call `get_weather(location="Tokyo", units="fahrenheit")`.
```

````text
4. `date`: "2020-13-45" is invalid. Month 13 does not exist, and day 45 does not exist.

| :--- | :--- | :--- |
| **`email`** | `"john@.com"` | **Invalid format.** The domain portion after `@` is missing a valid domain name (e.g., `john@.com` should be `john@something.com`). |
| **`age`** | `200` | **Out of range.** Exceeds the maximum plausible human age (typically capped at `120` or `150`). |
| **`phone`** | `"555-12"` | **Invalid format/length.** Too short and missing a proper area code or standard digit count (usually requires 7-10 digits). |
| **`date`** | `"2020-13-45"` | **Invalid date.** Month `13` and day `45` do not exist. Valid dates must fall within actual calendar constraints. |
| **`amount`** | `-50` | **Invalid value.** Negative numbers are typically rejected for monetary amounts or quantities, which should be `>= 0`. |

**Recommendation:** Update the payload to conform to standard formats before sending:
```json
{
  "email": "john@example.com",
  "age": 30,
  "phone": "555-123-4567",
  "date": "2020-01-15",
  "amount": 50.00
}
```

verdict=partial
summary=Found 3/5 errors. Missed some validation issues.
````

Implement server-side refresh token rotation (max 30 days).\n- CORS: Restrict staging wildcard origins to specific domains.\n- Webhooks: Implement request signing to prevent replay attacks.\n- Login rate limiting: Add progressive delays after 5 failed attempts.\n- Session management: Invalidate all active sessions upon password change.\n\n**4. Incident INC-4821 Resolution**\n- Root cause: Memory leak in connection pool manager (v2.14.3).\n- Impact: 50MB/hr growth causing 200-400ms GC pauses.\n- Fix: Switched to pooled connection factory with configurable idle/max lifetime.
